def local_identity(profile_id, principal_id, request_id):
    """The deterministic session id of a LOCAL creation receipt."""
    identity = json.dumps([profile_id, principal_id, request_id], separators=(',', ':'))
    return 'local-' + hashlib.sha256(identity.encode()).hexdigest()

def _generate_pkce() -> tuple:
    """Generate PKCE code_verifier and code_challenge (S256)."""
    verifier = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(secrets.token_bytes(32)).rstrip(b"=").decode()
    challenge = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(hashlib.sha256(verifier.encode()).digest()).rstrip(b"=").decode()
    return verifier, challenge

def deterministic_call_id(fn_name: str, arguments: str, index: int = 0) -> str:
    """Deterministic call_id fallback when the API omits one (random ids would break caching)."""
    seed = f"{fn_name}:{arguments}:{index}"
    return f"call_{hashlib.sha256(seed.encode('utf-8', errors='replace')).hexdigest()[:12]}"

def capability_set_hash(capabilities: Iterable[str]) -> str:

def _fs_etag(target: Path, st: os.stat_result) -> str:
    """A validator that changes whenever the file at *target* does."""
    identity = f"{target}\0{st.st_dev}\0{st.st_ino}\0{st.st_size}\0{st.st_mtime_ns}"
    return '"' + hashlib.sha256(identity.encode("utf-8", "surrogateescape")).hexdigest()[:32] + '"'

def message_context_id(scope: str, message_id: str) -> str:
    """The context a first message without a ``contextId`` opens. Named by the message, so a retry
    of that send after a timeout reopens the same context instead of a fresh one."""
    return "ctx-" + hashlib.sha256(f"{scope}\0{message_id}".encode()).hexdigest()[:16]

def _sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        for block in iter(lambda: f.read(_CHUNK), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def sha256_file(path: str) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def string_to_sign(canonical: str, date: str, scope: str) -> str:
    sts_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    return "\n".join(["AWS4-HMAC-SHA256", date, scope, sts_hash])

def file_sha256(path: str) -> str:

    return digest.hexdigest()

def record_hash(data: bytes) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256(data).digest()
    return "sha256=" + base64.urlsafe_b64encode(digest).rstrip(b"=").decode("ascii")

def _sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        for block in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        return hashlib.file_digest(stream, "sha256").hexdigest()

def shard_of(name: str, shards: int = SHARDS) -> int:

def digest(path: Path) -> str | None:
    """sha256 of a file's bytes (None when absent); symlinks are followed, as a reader would."""
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() if path.exists() else None

def _make_pkce() -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Return ``(verifier, challenge)`` — the desktop's PKCE pair."""
    verifier = _b64url_no_pad(b"desktop-verifier-secret-material-0123456789abcd")
    challenge = _b64url_no_pad(hashlib.sha256(verifier.encode("ascii")).digest())
    return verifier, challenge

def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(64 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)

=== OLD | C:\Users\sakha\AppData\Local\hermes\hermes-agent\tests\hermes_cli\test_session_recovery.py | Line 27 ===


def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(64 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def _digest(path):
    return {str(f.relative_to(path)): hashlib.sha256(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            for f in sorted(path.rglob("*")) if f.is_file() and ".git" not in f.parts and "node_modules" not in f.parts}

def _profile_digest(user_data_dir: str) -> str:
    """One profile dir, however it is spelled (a symlink, a trailing slash)."""
    return hashlib.sha256(os.path.realpath(user_data_dir).encode()).hexdigest()


def _same_profile(recorded, user_data_dir: str) -> bool:
    return isinstance(recorded, str) and os.path.realpath(recorded) == os.path.realpath(user_data_dir)

def ns_tag() -> str:
    """The name suffix of this namespace's records, leases and throwaway dirs (``""`` without an identity)."""
    identity = ns_identity()
    return f"-n{hashlib.sha256(identity.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]}" if identity else ""

def _hash_file(path: Path) -> Optional[str]:
    """Streaming sha256 of a file's bytes. None if unreadable/missing."""
    try:
        h = hashlib.sha256()
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(1 << 20), b""):
                h.update(chunk)
        return h.hexdigest()

def _url_digest(url: str, format: Optional[str], provider: str = "") -> str:
    # format AND provider are part of the key: html != markdown, and one backend's rendering is not another's.
    raw = f"{url}\n{format or 'markdown'}\n{provider or ''}"
    return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]

def _sha256_file(path: str) -> str:
    """Return hex SHA-256 digest of a file."""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(65536), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()

def local_identity(profile_id, principal_id, request_id):
    """The deterministic session id of a LOCAL creation receipt."""
    identity = json.dumps([profile_id, principal_id, request_id], separators=(',', ':'))

def _generate_pkce() -> tuple:
    """Generate PKCE code_verifier and code_challenge (S256)."""
    verifier = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(secrets.token_bytes(32)).rstrip(b"=").decode()
    challenge = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(hashlib.sha256(verifier.encode()).digest()).rstrip(b"=").decode()
    return verifier, challenge

def deterministic_call_id(fn_name: str, arguments: str, index: int = 0) -> str:
    """Deterministic call_id fallback when the API omits one (random ids would break caching)."""

def generate_pkce_pair() -> tuple[str, str]:
    """``(verifier, S256 challenge)``: 32 random bytes, RFC 7636 §4."""

def capability_set_hash(capabilities: Iterable[str]) -> str:
    """Deterministic sha256 over a capability set (order-insensitive)."""
    canon = "\n".join(sorted(set(capabilities)))
    return hashlib.sha256(canon.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

def _s256(verifier: str) -> str:
    """RFC 7636 S256 transform: base64url(sha256(ascii(verifier))), no padding."""
    digest = hashlib.sha256(verifier.encode("ascii")).digest()
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(digest).rstrip(b"=").decode("ascii")

def _refresh_provider(provider: DashboardAuthProvider, token: str) -> Session | None:
    # Keyed on the token alone: whoever presents this RT already owns the session, and a client
    # that changed network between two requests of one burst must still hit the cache.
    digest = hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).digest()
    key = (id(provider), digest)
    with _guard:

def _fs_etag(target: Path, st: os.stat_result) -> str:
    """A validator that changes whenever the file at *target* does."""
    identity = f"{target}\0{st.st_dev}\0{st.st_ino}\0{st.st_size}\0{st.st_mtime_ns}"
    return '"' + hashlib.sha256(identity.encode("utf-8", "surrogateescape")).hexdigest()[:32] + '"'

def message_context_id(scope: str, message_id: str) -> str:
    """The context a first message without a ``contextId`` opens. Named by the message, so a retry
    of that send after a timeout reopens the same context instead of a fresh one."""
    return "ctx-" + hashlib.sha256(f"{scope}\0{message_id}".encode()).hexdigest()[:16]

def _sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        for block in iter(lambda: f.read(_CHUNK), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def _input_digest(source: Path, importers: list[str]) -> str:
    # A coarse lookup hint, not a second receipt or dependency resolver. Owner
    # admission remains necessary even when this digest matches exactly.
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for name in input_files(source, importers):

        digest.update(path.read_bytes() if path.is_file() else b"<missing>")

def sha256_file(path: str) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def string_to_sign(canonical: str, date: str, scope: str) -> str:
    sts_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    return "\n".join(["AWS4-HMAC-SHA256", date, scope, sts_hash])

def _sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        for block in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
            digest.update(block)

def shard_of(name: str, shards: int = SHARDS) -> int:
    return int(hashlib.sha256(name.encode()).hexdigest()[:8], 16) % shards

def digest(path: Path) -> str | None:
    """sha256 of a file's bytes (None when absent); symlinks are followed, as a reader would."""
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() if path.exists() else None

def _make_pkce() -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Return ``(verifier, challenge)`` — the desktop's PKCE pair."""
    verifier = _b64url_no_pad(b"desktop-verifier-secret-material-0123456789abcd")
    challenge = _b64url_no_pad(hashlib.sha256(verifier.encode("ascii")).digest())
    return verifier, challenge

def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(64 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)

=== NEW | C:\Users\HermesAgent\AppData\Local\hermes\hermes-agent\tests\hermes_cli\test_session_recovery.py | Line 27 ===


def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(64 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def _digest(path):
    return {str(f.relative_to(path)): hashlib.sha256(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            for f in sorted(path.rglob("*")) if f.is_file() and ".git" not in f.parts and "node_modules" not in f.parts}

def _dm_delivery_id(dm_file: str | os.PathLike) -> str:
    """One delivery id per DM file: the dispatch ack, the live-owner intent and every retry
    of the runner derive it the same way, so the sender can correlate all of them."""

def _profile_digest(user_data_dir: str) -> str:
    """One profile dir, however it is spelled (a symlink, a trailing slash)."""
    return hashlib.sha256(os.path.realpath(user_data_dir).encode()).hexdigest()


def _same_profile(recorded, user_data_dir: str) -> bool:
    return isinstance(recorded, str) and os.path.realpath(recorded) == os.path.realpath(user_data_dir)

=== NEW | C:\Users\HermesAgent\AppData\Local\hermes\hermes-agent\tools\browser_launcher_holders.py | Line 54 ===
def ns_tag() -> str:
    """The name suffix of this namespace's records, leases and throwaway dirs (``""`` without an identity)."""
    identity = ns_identity()
    return f"-n{hashlib.sha256(identity.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]}" if identity else ""

=== NEW | C:\Users\HermesAgent\AppData\Local\hermes\hermes-agent\tools\checkpoint_manager.py | Line 327 ===
def _hash_file(path: Path) -> Optional[str]:
    """Streaming sha256 of a file's bytes. None if unreadable/missing."""
    try:
        h = hashlib.sha256()
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(1 << 20), b""):
                h.update(chunk)
        return h.hexdigest()

def _token_cache_key(token: str) -> str:
    """Stable non-reversible cache key for a bot token."""

def _store_blob(data: bytes) -> str:
    """Write *data* keyed by sha256 (existing blob left alone). Returns the hash."""

    dest = blobs_dir() / digest
    if not dest.exists():

        tmp = dest.with_name(f".tmp-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}-{digest}")

def _url_digest(url: str, format: Optional[str], provider: str = "") -> str:
    # format AND provider are part of the key: html != markdown, and one backend's rendering is not another's.
    raw = f"{url}\n{format or 'markdown'}\n{provider or ''}"
    return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]

=== NEW | C:\Users\HermesAgent\AppData\Local\hermes\hermes-agent\tools\web_result_cache.py | Line 290 ===
    # Hash the text as ``extract_cache_get`` reads it back: text-mode reads translate CRLF/CR to LF

def _sha256_file(path: str) -> str:
    """Return hex SHA-256 digest of a file."""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(65536), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()
